# Batch implementation plan

## 读取指南

先读本节的 current milestone / current batch state / next candidates；旧批次只在需要考古、验证历史决策或 release/debug 溯源时按 Batch ID 搜索 `docs/batch-history.md`。不要默认从 Batch 0 顺序重读 350+ 个历史批次。产品方向以 `docs/mission-control-product-direction.md` 为准，持续执行方式见 `docs/autonomous-goal.md`。

## 实施摘要

Batch 359 后，Go-owned/no-fallback public command surface、durable lanes、显式 reconcile、typed autonomy preflight、Mission brief / executor action、bounded reviewer dispatch → strict intake → verification-before-decision writeback → post-validation 的本机闭环、pack-memory promote/reconsume package E2E，以及 authorized execution observation evidence + bounded adapter execution report strict intake（含 authorized stopCondition boundaryHits、status summary enforcement、workspace-relative 与 case-relative machine-readable handoff）已形成底座。当前阶段继续从 contract/inventory field increments 转向 replaceable session executor / reviewer orchestration、lane executor / tool-adapter live validation、pack-memory product UX、Windows 本机 product-path 稳定与真实 release verification。

## 执行清单

### Batch 709：installed adapter live-validation recovery

状态：已完成实现、focused/package/full regressions、独立 correctness review、文档收尾、本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote inspection；implementation commit `3589b80` 已推送。Push run `30416964464` completed failure；Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `90465478541`/`90465478562`/`90465478609` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run view 30416964464 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90465478541`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，没有新的远程 signal，不声明 remote green。

目标：把 installed nested-cwd adapter product path 从 immutable receipt/report 已存在后的 provenance drift 接到 distinct reauthorization recovery，并让旧 attempt 与新 attempt 在 status、handoff、continue、`RESUME.md` 和 digest 中保持可区分的 durable lineage。

边界：不执行 adapter/heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed，不覆盖旧 receipt，不新增 public command、pack manifest 或 PowerShell runtime logic。repair/recovery 只发布只读指导或显式 hash-bound gate/receipt/evidence Apply；同一 gate 的 drift 继续 fail-closed，新的 attempt 必须使用不同 gate/receipt identity。

已实现内容：installed lifecycle E2E 现在在 receipt、双 hash observation 与 acknowledgement 后修改 report，验证 status 返回 `blocked-by-adapter-execution-provenance-drift`、不再暴露旧 receipt preview、WhatIf 不写 requests、returned Apply 产生 distinct gate；新 report/receipt/observation 使用新 gate namespace、session 与 receipt SHA。旧 receipt bytes 经 retry 前后相等，handoff/continue/`RESUME.md`/digest 同时保留两次 attempt 的 receipt path/SHA 与 session lineage。新增 `gate.ReadAdapterExecutionReceipt`，status/workstream 在 live report 无法按当前 gate 解释时从 canonical receipt 恢复 immutable lineage；已 acknowledgement closed 的 superseded attempt 不再被新 gate 的共用 report path 重新打开，而同一 gate drift 仍保持 fail-closed。

验证结果：installed recovery focused E2E 与 gate/workstream/CLI 受影响包回归通过；完整 `go test ./... -count=1` 最终复跑通过（CLI 249.519s），`go vet ./...` 通过，`GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go test -c ./internal/rekit/cli` 通过；`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-check -Format json` 返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release gate inventory ok`，`status`、`packs`、`doctor` 与 `git diff --check` 通过，后者仅有 Windows LF→CRLF working-copy warning。本批未改变 README、CLAUDE.md、reference、pack 配置或示例文档，因为改动只扩展现有 gate receipt/status/handoff durable lifecycle，且不改变 public command ABI 或 pack authoring contract；CHANGELOG、release readiness 与本 batch plan 已更新。独立 correctness review 两轮发现并关闭 malformed/bogus superseding identity 与 receipt fallback observation anchoring/status-workstream 一致性问题，最终复核无高置信残余。Implementation commit `3589b80` 已推送；push-triggered release run `30416964464` 的三平台 jobs 均 `steps=[]`，属于既有 runner/billing blocker，不声明 remote green。

### Current milestone

**Mission Commander operational closure and truthful release readiness**：把 durable lane/reviewer/autonomy contract 串成实际可运行、可跨会话接手、可验证的产品闭环，并区分 inventory ready、本地 gate executed 与远程 CI green。当前用户短期只要求 Windows 本机稳定可用；远程 Linux/macOS/Windows CI 因 runner/billing blocker 继续记录为 known gap，不阻塞本机 Mission Control 闭环。

### Batch 708：installed case-local adapter executor lifecycle

状态：已完成 installed case-local nested cwd product-path、focused/package/full regressions、独立 correctness review、文档收尾、本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote inspection；implementation commit `7439285` 已推送。Push run `30405139051` completed failure；macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90428784723`/`90428784750`/`90428784813` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run view 30405139051 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90428784723`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，没有新的远程 signal，不声明 remote green。

目标：从 installed `/rekit` thin shim 与 nested case cwd 走通 durable owner claim、authorized gate、外部 adapter report/artifacts、immutable receipt、replacement executor takeover、stale session fail-closed、observation、evidence acknowledgement 与 durable handoff/continue；让 replacement executor 从 durable artifacts 直接确认完整 provenance。

边界：不新增 public command、PowerShell runtime logic、pack manifest/schema 或 pack authoring contract；Go runtime 不执行 adapter/heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed；复用 `gate` receipt/validation/record 与 `note` verification WhatIf→hash-bound Apply。旧 owner/generation/session 不能采用 receipt，必须 fail-closed；新 owner 仍需显式 takeover 与最新 generation。

已实现内容：新增 isolated CLI fixture 内 action-specific `_template` tooling candidate、external report 与两个 case-relative artifact；从 nested cwd 消费 case metadata，显式 claim `installed-executor-a` 后创建 authorized gate，receipt preview 绑定 generation 1，再通过 `start -Apply` takeover 到 `installed-executor-b` generation 2，证明旧 receipt Apply 被拒绝且 canonical receipt 未创建；新 session receipt Apply、report validation、双 hash observation record、accepted verification acknowledgement、handoff/continue Apply 均通过。handoff、lane `RESUME.md` 与 continue digest 补齐 receipt path/SHA、execution owner、harness/session、catalog SHA 与 artifact count durable lineage。

验证结果：focused CLI/workstream adapter regressions通过；installed adapter 与相邻 shim/status tests `-count=3` 通过；`go test ./... -count=1` 通过（CLI 240.871s），`go vet ./...` 通过；Linux `GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go test -c` 覆盖 CLI/workstream；`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-check -Format json`、`status`、`packs`、`doctor` 与 `git diff --check` 通过，后者仅有 Windows LF→CRLF warning。独立 reviewer发现新增测试 cwd 未恢复，已改为复用 `fixture.chdir`，复核结论为无高置信残余。单独 `TestRunStatusJsonKit` 还发现 Batch 707 文档验证结果缺少完整本机 release minimum 命令文本，已修复 active handoff evidence，避免 completed release cadence 回退到重复本地验证动作。

### Batch 707：adapter execution provenance lifecycle

状态：已完成 runtime/CLI/test 实现、focused package、Windows 本机 CLI product-path、独立 correctness review、完整本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote inspection；implementation commit `6c86c7f` 已推送。Push run `30402872687` completed failure；macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90421581519`/`90421581611`/`90421581613` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run view 30402872687 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90421581519`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，没有新的远程 signal，不声明 remote green。

目标：把 strict authorized gate、selected adapter catalog、current durable lane executor generation、external harness/session、typed authorized/actual budget、outcome/exit status、report hash/bytes 与 output/evidence artifact hash/bytes 串成 immutable execution provenance。Go runtime 仍只记录并验证 external executor/harness observation，不执行 adapter 或 heavy tool。

边界：不新增 public command、PowerShell runtime logic或 authority/confirmed 写入；复用 `gate` 子模式。managed lane 当前 action存在 catalog candidate 时 receipt mandatory，未知/重复 candidate、owner/catalog/report/artifact drift、missing/directory/symlink/self-referencing artifact均 fail-closed；legacy 无 durable owner/catalog candidate 路径保持兼容。

已实现内容：新增 strict `adapterexecution` receipt contract、durable `laneowner` reader 与 `gate -RecordAdapterExecutionReceipt` preview→expected-binding-hash Apply。receipt 使用 canonical `.rekit/lanes/<lane>/adapter-executions/<gateEventId>/receipt.json` no-overwrite geometry，绑定 authorized gate snapshot、exact catalog candidate、current executor generation、harness/session、outcome/exit status、report及artifact hashes/bytes；exact replay不重写。read-only report validation在 managed path重验 receipt/current owner/catalog/report/artifacts，随后只发布 report+receipt双 hash-bound observation record command。observation、Mission Commander evidence review与authorized gate handoff投影 compact receipt lineage。

验证结果：focused/package/CLI product-path 回归覆盖 authorized gate Apply、report/artifact fixture、receipt preview→hash-bound Apply、receipt-backed validation、双 hash observation record、distinct provenance-drift reauthorization WhatIf→Apply、unknown/duplicate/malformed catalog candidate、catalog/report/artifact/owner/gate drift、invalid artifact types、temp-write failure recovery、receipt/observation exact replay、receipt mode flag allowlist及 reviewer packet目录时序隔离，并证明无 authority/confirmed 写入。独立 reviewer 三轮复核确认闭合 managed→legacy fail-open、receipt mode flag吞参、drift不可兑现 handoff与final-path partial write问题，最终无高置信残余。`go test ./... -count=1` 通过（CLI 238.201s），`go vet ./...` 通过；Linux `GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go test -c` 已覆盖 gate/CLI。`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-check -Format json` 返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release gate inventory ok`；`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command status`、`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command packs`、`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command doctor`、`go test ./...`、`go vet ./...` 与 `git diff --check` 通过，后者仅有 Windows LF→CRLF working-copy warning。该 `ready=true` 只表示本机 inventory ready；remote green仍待 implementation push 后检查。

### Batch 706：durable reviewer session receipt lifecycle

状态：已完成实现、focused/package/CLI product-path 回归、Linux test binary cross-compile、完整本机 release minimum、文档/CHANGELOG、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote inspection；implementation commit `699c8f8` 已推送。Push run `30391673831` completed failure；Windows/Linux/macOS jobs `90384524538`/`90384524680`/`90384524750` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run view 30391673831 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90384524538`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，没有新的远程 signal，不声明 remote green。

目标：把 immutable reviewer packet 接到真实 Claude Code harness session 生命周期，而不让 Go runtime 成为 Agent 进程管理器。主 Agent实际调用 read-only Agent tool 后记录 immutable dispatch receipt，session 结束后记录 immutable completion receipt；只有 current lane owner generation 下的 successful completion、exact reviewer session 和 exact result input hash/bytes 都匹配时，结果才可进入 source capture、staging、collection、intake 与 verification-before-decision writeback。

边界：不新增 public command、packet/board/facts schema 或 PowerShell runtime logic；复用 `plan-subagents` 子模式。Go runtime 不 spawn、poll、monitor、stop 或管理 reviewer session，receipt 只记录 harness observation；failed/partial/stale-owner attempt 不得进入 source capture，不执行 heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed。

已实现内容：新增共享 `reviewersession` strict receipt contract，以及 `-RecordReviewerDispatch` / `-RecordReviewerCompletion` 的 WhatIf→expected-hash Apply 路径。dispatch receipt 绑定 packet/route/shard/items、prompt SHA-256、harness/session、lane owner/adoption generation；completion receipt 绑定 dispatch receipt SHA-256、outcome/exit status、current completion owner 与 exact result input path/hash/bytes。receipt 使用 deterministic dispatch ID、canonical case-local no-overwrite geometry、strict single-object JSON、exact replay 幂等与 conflicting replay fail-closed。status/handoff/continue/operator package 现在投影 ready-to-dispatch、running-unknown、completion-preview、failed、stale-owner、invalid、completed/source-capture states；prompt/input/source/candidate/recovery integrity blocker 保持更高优先级。source capture 与 intake 均重验 current owner 和 receipt lineage，verification/decision events 记录 harness、dispatch/completion receipt及input SHA provenance。CLI product path 覆盖 dispatch/completion preview/apply、takeover stale attempt、failed attempt、session mismatch、successful capture/intake/writeback。

验证结果：focused reviewer session、takeover/re-adoption、same-session multi-attempt、managed receipt bypass、completion/dispatch lineage tamper、post-collection input removal、multi-packet priority、batch intake、recovery/obstruction/disposition CLI product-path 与 Batch 706 package regressions通过；独立 reviewer 三轮复核发现并确认闭合 managed bypass、multi-attempt、completion lineage、post-collection input 与 dispatch tamper correctness 问题，最终无高置信残余。`GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go test -c` 已覆盖 subagents/workstream。`go test ./... -count=1` 通过（CLI 235.807s），`go vet ./...` 通过；`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-check -Format json` 返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release gate inventory ok`，`status`、`packs`、`doctor` 与 `git diff --check` 通过，后者仅有 Windows LF→CRLF working-copy warning。该 `ready=true` 只表示本机 inventory ready；remote green 仍待 implementation push 后检查。

### Batch 705：reviewer writeback ordering and exact recovery move

状态：已完成实现、focused/package/CLI 回归、Windows handle-bound 竞态验证、Linux test binary cross-compile、完整本机 release minimum、文档/CHANGELOG、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote inspection；implementation commit `643df26` 已推送。Push run `30377288105` completed failure；Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `90336096385`/`90336096542`/`90336096584` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run view 30377288105 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90336096385`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，没有新的远程 signal，不声明 remote green。

目标：修复 reviewer verification/decision writeback 跨 ledger 的伪 `Latest*` 语义，并关闭 regular-file reviewer recovery 在 stable read 后、path-based rename 前的 source replacement 窗口。恢复动作必须证明被验证的 source object 就是被 quarantine 的 object；无法提供这种原语的平台不得发布可执行 recovery handoff。

边界：不新增 public command、durable schema 或 PowerShell runtime logic；不自动 spawn reviewer、不执行 heavy tool、不写 authority/confirmed。Windows 复用既有 source-handle + namespace guard + destination directory handle + no-replace `NtSetInformationFile` primitive；非 Windows 在 mutation 前 fail-closed，不把 `renameat` 或 rename 后 hash check 声称为 exact move。

已实现内容：`ReviewerWritebackItems` 现在合并 verification/decision events 后按有效 RFC3339Nano `createdAt` 全局稳定排序，再保留最新 `maxHandoffRows`；legacy 无/非法时间事件保持确定性 append order 并排在可信时间事件之前。reviewer recovery 底层 primitive 泛化为 regular/empty-file/symlink exact move：Windows 在同一不共享 write/delete 的 source handle 上校验类型、大小和完整 bytes，再对该 handle 执行 destination no-replace rename；collection/recovery preview 根据平台 capability 决定是否发布 recovery action，非 Windows 返回 unsupported 且不改 source/quarantine/intent。新增 source replacement、unexpected handle bytes、existing quarantine no-replace、UNC handle path 规范化、跨 ledger latest/global limit/legacy fallback 回归，并让跨平台测试显式区分 supported closure 与 unsupported fail-closed。

验证结果：focused reviewer writeback、subagents recovery/exact-move、workstream package与 CLI reviewer/status regressions 已通过；Windows source identity、pre-move bytes、destination no-replace tests 已通过；`GOOS=linux GOARCH=amd64 go test -c ./internal/rekit/subagents` 通过。最终 `go test ./... -count=1` 通过（CLI 259.145s），`go vet ./...` 通过。`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-check -Format json` 返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release gate inventory ok`，`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command status`、`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command packs`、`go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command doctor` 与 `git diff --check` 已通过；`git diff --check` 仅保留 Windows 工作树 LF→CRLF 提示。Implementation commit `643df26` 已推送；push run `30377288105` completed failure，Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `90336096385`/`90336096542`/`90336096584` 均 `steps=[]` 且无失败日志，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker，不声明 remote green。

### Batch 702：pack-memory proof closure to durable project handoff

状态：已完成 runtime/CLI/test 改动、文档/CHANGELOG、完整本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `5e5ad0f` 已推送。Push run `30345373009` completed failure；macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90230220472`/`90230220579`/`90230220606` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run view 30345373009 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90230220472`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。本批从 reviewer orchestration 转向 authorized execution evidence / adapter live validation：authorized gate adapter report scaffold/draft 已有 hash-bound preview/apply 和 `already-scaffolded` / `already-drafted` 预览语义，但 replacement executor 重跑同一个 exact-existing `-ScaffoldExecutionReport -Apply` 或 `-DraftExecutionReport -Apply` 时，runtime 只返回 `AlreadyExists` 且 `Applied=false`，terminal text 也没有 `replay`，容易把已存在 sidecar 误读为未完成写入、重复 rerun adapter，或把 scaffold/draft replay 与真正 evidence record 混淆。

目标：让 authorized gate adapter report scaffold/draft duplicate Apply 成为可消费的 idempotent replay handoff。重复执行同一 expected SHA-256 的 scaffold/draft Apply，且目标 `adapter-report.json` bytes 与 deterministic scaffold/draft 完全一致时，必须返回 `mode=already-scaffolded` / `mode=already-drafted`、`applied=true`、`replay=true`、`alreadyExists=true`，不重写 sidecar bytes，不写 observations/authority/confirmed，不 record evidence，不执行 adapter/heavy tool。Mission Commander action/queue 必须直接指向 read-only validation，next steps 与 text output 明确这是 duplicate Apply replay、did not rewrite bytes、do not rerun adapter，并保留 hash-bound record 只能来自 validation/status `valid=true` envelope 的边界。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 authorized gate request、adapter report sidecar、execution evidence ledger 或 facts schema，不放宽 expected-hash Apply，不覆盖 different-bytes sidecar，不自动 scaffold/draft/validate/record，不执行 adapter或 heavy tool，不写 observations/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。scaffold/draft replay 只确认 exact sidecar 已存在并移交 read-only validation；真正 observation evidence 仍必须通过 separate validation returned hash-bound record command。

已实现内容：`gate.AdapterExecutionReportScaffold` 与 `gate.AdapterExecutionReportDraft` 新增 optional `replay`。`ScaffoldAdapterExecutionReport` / `DraftAdapterExecutionReport` 在 Apply 路径命中 exact-existing sidecar 时，现在返回 `Applied=true`、`Replay=true`，保留 `Mode=already-scaffolded` / `already-drafted` 与 `AlreadyExists=true`，重建 Mission Commander validation handoff、action queue、runbook 和 replay next steps；首次 scaffold/draft Apply 仍保持 `mode=scaffolded` / `drafted`、`replay=false` 并写入或替换 exact scaffold。CLI text summary 同步输出 `replay`。扩展 gate unit regressions 和 nested output workspace CLI product-path E2E：覆盖 duplicate scaffold/draft JSON Apply、duplicate text Apply、bytes unchanged、zero facts mutation、validation-only Mission Commander handoff，并同步更新既有 adapter report text 输出断言。

### Batch 695：reviewer packet retirement replay closure

目标：让 invalid reviewer packet retirement duplicate Apply 成为可消费的 replay handoff。重复执行同一 expected packet/integrity SHA-256 的 `plan-subagents -RetireInvalidReviewerPacket -Apply` 时，必须返回 `mode=already-retired` / `replay=true`，不重写 `packet.retirement.json`，next steps 明确不要从 retired packet dispatch/collect/intake/adopt/continue，只能 rerun status、重新生成 canonical reviewer packet 或在确认 reviewer blocker absent 后继续 lane。JSON 与 text 都必须可消费；status/handoff/continue 仍必须保持 closed-provenance retirement handoff，并且 fresh packet regeneration 只提升新 packet 的 dispatch。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 reviewer packet/result/facts schema，不删除或修复 retired packet/integrity bytes，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不自动 dispatch/collect/intake/adopt，不执行 heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。唯一 runtime 改动是 duplicate exact retirement receipt 的返回级 replay/mode handoff；durable receipt 语义保持不变。

已实现内容：`subagents.ReviewerPacketRetirementResult` 新增 `replay`。`RetireInvalidReviewerPacket` 在 Apply 路径读取到 matching `packet.retirement.json` 时，现在返回 `mode=already-retired`、`replay=true` 与 closed-provenance next steps；首次 Apply 仍保持 `mode=reviewer-packet-retirement`、`replay=false` 并写入 receipt。CLI text summary 同步输出 `mode` 与 `replay`。扩展 `TestRunPlanSubagentsReviewerPacketRetirementWhatIfApplyE2E`：首次 Apply 后保存 receipt bytes，重跑同一 JSON Apply 并断言 `already-retired` / replay / no-dispatch next steps，再重跑 text Apply 证明 terminal 输出包含 replay handoff，并验证 receipt bytes 未变化；后续既有 status/handoff/continue/regenerated fresh packet 断言继续证明 retired packet 没有被重开。

### Batch 694：stale owner-guard takeover-to-continue product-path closure

状态：已完成产品路径回归、文档/CHANGELOG、本机验证、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `c034180` 已推送。Push run `30341778077` completed failure；Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `90218766809`/`90218766863`/`90218766882` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run view 30341778077 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90218766809`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。Batch 692 已让 stale/missing owner guard 的 `continue` 返回 fail-closed / zero-write recovery envelope，Batch 693 已让 `status` first-screen 直接投影当前 lane takeover package；但 replacement executor 从旧会话 stale `continue` 接手时，产品路径测试仍停在“看到 recovery”和“current executor 可继续”，没有证明 executor 按 recovery guidance 执行 explicit `start` takeover preview/apply 后，会获得新 generation 的 owner-bound continue，并且旧 executor 再次尝试 `continue` 会继续 fail-closed 指向新 owner。

目标：把 stale `continue` recovery → explicit `start -WhatIf -Executor <new>` takeover preview → `start -Apply -Executor <new>` takeover → old executor 二次 stale `continue` recovery → new executor owner-bound `continue -Apply` 串成一个 CLI 产品路径回归。该回归必须证明 recovery 阶段 zero-write，takeover preview read-only 且给出 apply-required `LaneTakeoverPackage`，takeover apply durable 更新 current executor/generation 与 ready continue package，二次 stale recovery 指向最新 executor/generation，最终 continue apply 只接受新 owner guard。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 owner guard、lane/autonomy/reviewer/gate/adapter/pack-memory durable schema，不自动 claim executor、不自动 takeover、不自动 continue，不执行 reviewer/adapter/pack-memory/gate/sync/promote/heavy-tool mutation，不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。若 runtime 已满足链路，则只补产品路径回归与文档，不制造无必要 runtime 改动。

已实现内容：扩展 `TestRunContinueReplaceableSessionOwnerGuardNestedProductPath`。测试现在在 `session-a` stale recovery 和 `session-b` current preview 之后，继续从 nested case-local cwd 运行 recovery-guided `start login -WhatIf -Executor session-c`，断言 preview zero-write、generation 递增到 3、top-level Mission Commander follow-up 指向 `/rekit continue login -Executor session-c -ExpectedExecutorGeneration 3`，且 apply-required `LaneTakeoverPackage.currentCommand` 指向 explicit takeover Apply。随后运行 `start login -Apply -Executor session-c`，断言 lane durable executor/generation/lastTakeover provenance 更新、writes 包含 `update-executor-takeover`，并且 ready package 输出新 owner-bound continue command。测试再用旧 `session-b` generation 2 重跑 `continue -WhatIf`，断言仍 fail-closed / zero-write 且 recovery 指向 `session-c` generation 3；最后用 `session-c` generation 3 执行 `continue -Apply`，证明 takeover 后的 replacement executor 可直接继续。

### Batch 693：Mission Commander first-screen lane takeover package closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 实现、本机验证、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `6141c79` 已推送。Push run `30340190330` completed failure；macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90213804401`/`90213804403`/`90213804435` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run view 30340190330 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90213804401`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。Batch 691 已让 `start`、lane `handoff`、`continue`、`RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 都暴露 `LaneTakeoverPackage`，Batch 692 已让 stale/missing owner guard 的 `continue` 返回 structured recovery envelope。本批继续补齐 replacement executor 从 case-local `/rekit` shim、nested cwd 或新会话第一步运行 `status` 的 first-screen 接手断点：当 Mission Commander first screen 聚焦某条 lane 的 ready/blocked current action 时，第一屏直接展示同源 takeover package，而不是只给 generic action/reason/boundary。

目标：当 `status -Format json/text` 的 Mission Commander first-screen focus 选中 lane current action（ready-to-continue、blocked lane takeover、reviewer/adapter/gate/intervention blocker 等仍属于 lane focus）时，必须直接投影 read-only `LaneTakeoverPackage` 或等价 first-screen takeover package。该 package 要与 `start`/`handoff`/`continue` 同源，包含 lane identity/status/workspace/currentExecutor/executorGeneration、resume/checkpoint/handoff paths、owner-bound continue/handoff/current commands、Mission Commander current action/queue、runbook 与 boundary；nested case-local shim product path 也必须可见。JSON 与 text 输出都必须可消费；first-screen projection 必须 zero-mutation，不执行 continue/handoff/start/gate/reviewer/adapter/pack-memory/heavy action，不写 facts/authority/confirmed。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 claim executor、不自动 takeover、不自动 run continue，不改变 owner guard、lane/autonomy/reviewer/gate/adapter/pack-memory durable schema，不把 status projection 当授权或 confirmed，不执行 heavy-tool mutation，不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。first-screen package 只是 read-only guidance；真正接手仍必须使用 explicit `start -Apply` 或 current owner-bound `continue` command。

已实现内容：`workstream` 新增 exported read-only `LaneTakeoverPackageForActionSnapshot`，从 existing `mission.LaneExecutorActionSnapshot` 和 Mission Commander action queue 构造同源 `LaneTakeoverPackage`，复用既有路径、owner-bound continue/handoff/current command、runbook 与 boundary 逻辑，不在 CLI 复制 package builder。`statusCaseMission` 新增 `firstScreenLaneTakeoverPackage` JSON 字段；`buildStatusCaseMission` 在 overview inventory 现有 lane executor snapshots 和 current action queue 上构造当前 first-screen lane package。CLI `status -Format text` 在 `case-current-action` focus 分支先输出 `status Mission Commander focus takeover package` summary/paths/commands/commander/current/runbook/boundary，再输出 authorized-gate specialized package，确保 blocked lane（reconcile/pending gate/open decision）与 ready lane 都能从第一屏拿到 current executor/generation、safe handoff/resume/checkpoint paths 和 no-heavy/no-authority/no-auto-continue boundary。

### Batch 692：stale lane takeover recovery closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 实现、本机验证、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `7757da9` 已推送。Push run `30338724558` completed failure；Windows/Linux/macOS jobs `90209242653`/`90209242664`/`90209242762` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run view 30338724558 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90209242653`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。Batch 691 已把成功接手路径收敛成 `LaneTakeoverPackage`；本批继续补齐 replacement executor 拿着旧 handoff、旧 `RESUME.md` 或旧聊天中的 stale / missing `-Executor` / `-ExpectedExecutorGeneration` 运行 `continue` 时的恢复闭环：strict owner guard 仍 fail-closed 阻止错误 executor 写入，但返回可消费的 structured recovery envelope，而不是只返回裸 error。

目标：在 `continue` owner guard mismatch / missing owner guard / legacy unassigned mismatch 路径中新增 read-only stale takeover recovery envelope。该 envelope 必须保持 fail-closed 和 zero-write，不放宽 owner guard；但要返回 current lane owner/generation、received executor/generation、resume/checkpoint/handoff paths、safe handoff command、explicit start takeover preview/apply command template、current owner-bound continue command、Mission Commander current action/queue、runbook 与 boundary。JSON 与 text 输出都必须可消费；CLI/product-path regression 应覆盖旧 generation continue 失败、缺 guard continue 失败，以及恢复 envelope 指向 current `LaneTakeoverPackage` / handoff / explicit takeover，而不写 run artifacts、facts、authority 或 confirmed。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 claim executor、不自动 takeover、不自动 run continue，不把 stale guard 当成功预览，不执行 reviewer/adapter/pack-memory/gate/sync/promote/heavy-tool mutation，不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不改变 existing strict owner guard 语义，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。recovery package 只是 fail-closed guidance；真正接手仍必须使用 explicit `start -Apply -Executor ... -Actor ... -Reason ...`，后续 continue 仍必须带当前 owner guard。

已实现内容：`internal/rekit/workstream` 新增 `ContinueOwnerGuardRecovery` DTO、runbook 与 boundary builder，并把 `ContinueResult` 接入 optional `continueOwnerGuardRecovery`。`ContinuePreview` 与 `ContinueApply` 现在通过 recovery-aware context 保留原 strict `validateContinueOwnerBinding` 判断；当 owner guard missing、generation missing、stale executor/generation 或 legacy unassigned explicit mismatch 时返回 `Blocked=true` / `Applied=false` / zero-write recovery result，而不获取 run artifacts、写 facts 或继续 lane。recovery envelope 同源嵌入当前 `LaneTakeoverPackage`，并暴露 received/current executor generation、current owner-bound continue command、handoff/resume/checkpoint paths、explicit `start -WhatIf/-Apply -Executor <new-executor>` takeover command template、Mission Commander current action/queue、runbook 与 no-heavy/no-authority/no-auto-takeover boundary。CLI `continue` text 输出新增 owner-guard recovery summary/received/current/paths/takeover/commander/runbook/boundary，并在 recovery 分支避免打印普通 continue preview/apply 成功文案。

### Batch 691：lane takeover package closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused lane takeover regressions、相邻 workstream/CLI regressions、完整 CLI package regression、完整本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `9d68b2f` 已推送。Push run `30336292452` completed failure；macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90201795151`/`90201795161`/`90201795208` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run watch 30336292452 --exit-status` annotations 显示 GitHub account payments/spending limit blocker，`gh run view 30336292452 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90201795151`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。本批选择 replacement executor / Mission Commander operational closure 交界 slice，推进一个 Windows 本机可验证的 durable lane takeover product path。Batch 672–690 已持续把 owner guard、reviewer/adapter/pack-memory/release handoff 与 next-batch starter package投影到不同入口；但 replacement executor 真正接手某条 lane 时，仍需要在 `start` result、`handoff <lane>` JSON/Markdown、`continue` preview、lane `RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 之间手工拼接“当前 executor 是谁、generation guard 是多少、下一条 owner-bound continue 命令是什么、Mission Commander current action/queue 是什么、哪些 boundary 禁止自动执行”。这会让 lane takeover 依赖旧聊天上下文或多个 artifact 串读，尤其在 executor replacement 和 reviewer/adapter blocker 混合时容易漏掉 owner guard 或继续条件。

目标：新增同源 read-only `LaneTakeoverPackage`，让 `start -WhatIf/-Apply`、lane `handoff -WhatIf/-Apply`、`continue -WhatIf/-Apply`、lane `RESUME.md` 与 `.rekit/lanes/<lane>/checkpoints/latest.json` 都暴露同一 takeover envelope。package 必须包含 lane identity/status/workspace/currentExecutor/executorGeneration/lastTakeover provenance、resume/checkpoint/handoff paths、owner-bound continue/handoff/current commands、applyRequired/blocked/continueReady、Mission Commander action/current action/action queue、runbook steps 与 no-heavy/no-authority/owner-guard boundary。start preview 的 package 必须明确 `applyRequired=true`，start apply / handoff / continue 的 package 必须能作为 replacement executor 的 durable takeover packet 使用。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 lane/autonomy/reviewer/gate/adapter/pack-memory durable schema 的既有 mutation 语义，不自动 claim executor、不自动 run continue、不执行 reviewer/adapter/pack-memory/gate/sync/promote/heavy-tool mutation，不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。`LaneTakeoverPackage` 只是 read-only takeover envelope；真正接手仍必须走 explicit `start -Apply`，继续执行仍必须保留 `-Executor` 与 `-ExpectedExecutorGeneration` owner guard，blocked lanes 仍必须先解除 Mission Commander current action。

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused CLI authorized-gate first-screen product-path regression、完整 CLI package regression、完整本机 release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `fb86df6` 已推送。Push run `30325667424` completed failure；macOS/Windows/Linux jobs `90170458127`/`90170458176`/`90170458194` 均 `steps=[]`；`gh run view 30325667424 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90170458127`。这是既有 runner/billing blocker，未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明 remote green。本批选择 Batch 685 暴露的 `authorized-evidence` / `adapter-live-validation` candidate-domain，推进一个 Windows 本机可验证的 Mission Commander / lane executor product-path slice。当前 authorized-gate adapter contract、live validation、repair hints 与 hash-bound record command 已在 `status` detail、`handoff`、`continue` 和 gate contract/validation 输出中可见；但 Mission Commander first-screen 在 `case-current-action` 聚焦 adapter action 时仍只输出 generic current action/reason/boundary，replacement executor 若只看第一屏，还要继续向下查找 authorized gate handoff 才能拿到 report path、workspace-relative/case-relative validate/scaffold/draft/record commands、repair hints 与 no-heavy/no-authority boundary。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 gate/adapter report/ledger durable schema，不执行 heavy-tool、不运行 adapter、不自动 scaffold/draft/validate/record、不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。first-screen 仅是 read-only projection；mutation 仍必须通过既有 explicit WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或 validation 返回的 hash-bound record command。

### Batch 677：managed reviewer dispatch packet closure

状态：已完成 managed reviewer dispatch packet closure 的 runtime/test 实现、focused subagents plan regression、focused CLI reviewer orchestration product-path regression 与相邻 reviewer regressions；文档、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection 已完成；implementation commit `a53f482` 已推送。Push run `30296169199` completed failure，Windows/Linux/macOS jobs `90077662468`/`90077662491`/`90077662556` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs；`gh run watch` annotations 显示 account payments/spending limit blocker，`gh run view 30296169199 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90077662468`，仍是既有 GitHub Actions runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批从 `release-check` known gap 中的 “Mission Commander 尚无统一 session/reviewer orchestrator” 选择一个 Windows 本机可验证的中型产品 slice：`plan-subagents` 已能生成 reviewer orchestration plan、prompt artifacts、owner binding 与 strict intake/collection commands，但 replacement executor 仍需要从多处分散字段手工拼接“该发给哪些 reviewer、每个 reviewer 的 prompt/hash/result/input/collection/intake 下一步是什么、哪些动作是禁止的”。Batch 677 将这些内容收敛成 packet/summary 中的 managed dispatch packet：一份 read-only、可校验、按 shard 排序的托管 dispatch 清单，供 Mission Commander 或 replacement executor 直接接手分发与收集 reviewer 输出；runtime 仍不 spawn、不 monitor、不 stop reviewer，不执行 Agent tool，不写 authority/confirmed。

目标：attached case 中 `plan-subagents` 生成多 shard reviewer plan 后，JSON result、canonical packet 与 `summary.md` 都必须提供同一份 managed dispatch packet：包含 target lane、owner binding、packet/prompt/result roots、reviewer count/maxParallel、per-shard dispatch manifest（prompt path/sha256、Agent tool request、expected reviewer JSON skeleton、source capture/staging/collection/intake command chain、read-only/no-heavy/no-authority boundary）以及 packet-level batch intake preview/apply。replacement executor 只看该 managed packet 即可按序 dispatch read-only reviewer、保存 JSON、执行 hash-gated source capture/staging/collection 与 batch intake preview/apply；普通 `status`/`handoff` 后续仍由既有 reviewer dispatch/intake handoff 消费 packet artifacts，不新增 public command 或 durable schema migration。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动启动 Claude Code reviewer/subagent，不调用 Agent tool，不执行 reviewer 工作、不 source capture/stage/collect/intake、不继续 lane、不执行 heavy tool，不写 facts/authority/confirmed，不改变 reviewer packet/result/intake durable schema，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。managed dispatch packet 是 read-only handoff/manifest，所有 mutation 仍来自既有 explicit WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或测试临时 case artifacts。

已实现内容：`ReviewerOrchestrationPlan` 新增 optional `managedDispatchPacket`，其 summary 暴露 `managedDispatchSummary`；packet 按 shard 收敛 prompt path/SHA-256、Agent tool request、reviewer result skeleton、source capture input/source path、staging preview、collection preview/apply、per-shard intake preview/apply、packet-level batch preview/apply、runbook、completion criteria 与 no-spawn/no-heavy/no-authority boundary。`summary.md` 新增 `managed reviewer dispatch packet` 区块，CLI text 新增 `plan-subagents managed reviewer dispatch ...` 行，确保 replacement executor 在终端或 packet 中都能看到同一份托管 dispatch 清单。`managedDispatchPacket` 作为 optional read-only handoff 字段存在，不改变 public command surface，也不让 runtime 自动启动 reviewer 或执行 intake；旧无 orchestration packet identity 兼容路径仍由 regression 锁定。

### Batch 674：authorized adapter max-row survivorship closure

状态：已完成 authorized adapter max-row survivorship closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused 本机验证、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `13411aa` 已推送。Push run `30288953357` completed failure，Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `90053674974`/`90053675023`/`90053675118` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 GitHub Actions runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。Batch 673 release inspection cadence 已完成，implementation commit `ff6ff04` 与 release inspection commit `e932ac9` 已推送；其 push-triggered remote run 仍是既有 GitHub Actions runner/billing `steps=[]` blocker，不为 inspection commit 自己触发的 CI 追加第三个记录，且不声明远程绿色。

目标：当同一 case/lane 存在超过 `maxHandoffRows` 个 authorized gates，且较早 gate 仍有 adapter live validation repair / record / validation action、较新 gates 只是低价值 recorded/acknowledged/default provenance 时，`status`、handoff、continue 与 Mission Commander action queue 必须保留可解除 execution evidence closure 的高价值 adapter handoff，而不是先用 `lastObjects` 丢弃较早 actionable gate。replacement executor 只看第一屏即可继续修复/记录最关键 adapter report，同时仍保持 low-value 或 acknowledged gate 为 read-only provenance/可丢弃项。

已实现内容：`AuthorizedGateAdapterHandoffsWithAcknowledgements` 不再先对 raw authorized-gate requests 使用 `lastObjects(items, maxHandoffRows)`，而是先扫描全部 authorized gates、构造 live adapter handoff、应用 acknowledgement，再用 lifecycle-aware `limitAuthorizedGateAdapterHandoffs` 限制行数；priority 为 main escalation → `repair-adapter-report` → `ready-to-record-evidence` → validation/scaffold/draft waiting → scaffold/draft apply → ordinary/default → recorded provenance → acknowledged provenance，同 priority 保留较新的 handoff。`status` 的 authorized gate handoff JSON 也从完整 ledger requests 构造，并用同类 priority limit，避免 first-screen Mission Commander queue 已保留 repair action 但 `authorizedGateHandoffs[]` 列表本身仍被 overview 最近行截断。CLI no-pack nested output workspace product-path E2E 在旧 gate 已 acknowledged、新 gate repair handoff 存在后追加 5 个较新的 authorized overflow gates，覆盖 `status` JSON/text、`handoff main` WhatIf/Apply JSON/text、`continue main` WhatIf/Apply JSON/text、run `status.json` 与 `digest.md`，断言较早 repair handoff 仍保留且 current action 仍为 `move-evidence-refs-under-authorized-output-paths`，同时 oldest lower-priority overflow handoff 会被丢弃。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动执行 adapter/heavy tool，不自动 repair/validate/record/acknowledge，不删除或覆盖 sidecar，不改变 authorized-gate / execution report / acknowledgement durable schema，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。所有 mutation 仍必须由既有 gate/note/handoff/continue 的显式 WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或测试临时 fixture 触发；本批只修正 read-only authorized adapter handoff survivorship 与 action queue projection。

### Batch 673：pack-memory multi-pack lifecycle priority closure

状态：已完成 pack-memory multi-pack lifecycle priority closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused releasecheck pack-memory ordering regression、相邻 CLI/status project handoff regression、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `ff6ff04` 已推送。Push run `30286060677` completed failure，macOS/Windows/Linux jobs `90044033876`/`90044034073`/`90044034091` 均 `steps=[]`，`gh run watch` annotations 明确 jobs 未启动原因是 account payments/spending limit，`gh run view 30286060677 --log-failed` 返回 `log not found: 90044033876`，仍是既有 GitHub Actions runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批把多个 pack 同时存在 pack-memory candidate / decision receipt / verification / cleanup / proof follow-up work 时的 Mission Commander current action 选择，从 manifest pack 顺序 / action 生成顺序收敛为 pack-memory lifecycle closure priority。replacement executor 在较早 pack 仍处于普通 proof/review，较晚 pack 已到 verification retirement，或另一个 pack 有 in-progress provisioning 需要恢复时，第一屏会先显示最靠近 downstream closure 或最需要恢复的 pack-memory current action，同时保留其它 pack 的 read-only provenance；proof follow-up 仍作为 follow-up，不替代 primary current action。

目标：当多个 pack 同时存在 pack-memory candidate / decision receipt / verification / cleanup / proof follow-up work 时，`release-check`、`status` 与 project handoff 的 Mission Commander action queue 必须按 pack-memory lifecycle closure priority 选择 current action，而不是按 manifest pack 顺序或 action 生成顺序选择较早 pack。下游或可恢复阶段（retirement in-progress、provisioning in-progress、retirement required、verification run、provision required）应优先于普通 proof / review / cleanup open work；proof follow-up 仍保持 follow-up，不替代 primary current action。replacement executor 只看第一屏即可先恢复/关闭最靠近 downstream closure 的 pack-memory work，并保留其它 pack 的 read-only provenance。

已实现内容：`packMemoryCandidateNextActions` 现在在生成 per-pack primary 与 proof follow-up actions 后调用 `orderPackMemoryCandidateActions`，再进入 `MissionCommanderActionQueueFor`。新 priority 稳定排序为 verification retirement in-progress → provisioning in-progress → retirement required → verification run required → provision required → cleanup proof → reconsume proof → decision proof → decision draft → cleanup/open/ready/default；`.followUp.proof` actions 在同一 lifecycle priority 基础上追加 follow-up offset，确保 cleanup/reconsume/decision proof follow-up 继续留在 follow-up 队列，不遮蔽 primary current action。新增 releasecheck unit regression 构造两个/三个 pack：manifest 顺序较早的 proof-required pack、较晚的 retirement-required pack 与 provisioning-in-progress pack，断言 current action 分别选中 retirement closure 或 in-progress provisioning resume。新增 CLI/status regression 通过 `buildStatusProjectHandoff` 与 first-screen focus path 证明排序后的 pack-memory queue 会进入 `status` project handoff，并让第一屏聚焦 `pack-memory-current-action`。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 merge/cleanup/provision/verify/retire/reconsume，不改变 pack-memory candidate decision receipt / verification proof / lifecycle proof durable schema，不执行 heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。所有 mutation 仍必须由既有 `promote` WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或测试临时 fixture 显式触发；本批只修正 read-only Mission Commander action queue ordering 与 product-path projection。

### Batch 672：reviewer owner-adoption durable continuation closure

状态：已完成 reviewer owner-adoption durable continuation closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused reviewer owner-adoption workstream regression、focused reviewer owner-adoption CLI product-path regression、相邻 reviewer/status regressions、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `c69971f` 已推送。Push run `30284477328` completed failure，macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `90038741684`/`90038741755`/`90038741787` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批把 reviewer packet ownerBinding 过期后的 replacement executor 接手从“先 adoption Apply，再手工推断 packet 已可 intake”收敛为 durable continuation：current adoption receipt 通过 strict packet/hash/current-executor 校验后，`status`、lane `handoff` 与 blocked `continue -Apply` 都继续把 reviewer dispatch/intake handoff 投影为 `ready-for-reviewer-intake-preview`，同时保留 adoption receipt path、actor/reason/createdAt、current executor/generation、evidence、runbook 与 no-spawn/no-heavy/no-authority boundary；blocked continue 仍保持 zero-write、`run-preview`、不创建 run artifacts。

目标：构造 Windows 本机可验证的 reviewer owner adoption product-path：同一 feature lane 先由 `session-a` 生成 reviewer packet 并完成 reviewer result capture/staging/collection，随后 replacement executor `session-b` 接管 lane，旧 packet ownerBinding stale 时必须先暴露 `reviewer-packet-owner-adoption-required`，且 direct ready-results intake fail-closed、不写 facts。执行 explicit `-AdoptReviewerPacket` WhatIf → Apply 后，immutable packet bytes 不变；replacement executor 只看 `status -Format json`、lane `handoff -WhatIf -Format json` 或 owner-guarded `continue review -Executor session-b -ExpectedExecutorGeneration 2 -Apply -Format json`，都能看到 current adoption receipt 已接受、下一步为 packet-level `-ReadyReviewerResults -WhatIf`，并保留 reviewer intake Apply 前必须 review 的 boundary。

### Batch 671：reviewer multi-packet lifecycle priority closure

状态：已完成 reviewer multi-packet lifecycle priority closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused reviewer workstream regression、focused reviewer multi-packet CLI product-path regression、相邻 reviewer/status regressions、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `a66e444` 已推送。Push run `30282103650` completed failure，macOS/Windows/Linux jobs `90030791940`/`90030791944`/`90030792147` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批把多个 reviewer packet 或同一 packet 多个 shard 并存时的 Mission Commander current action 选择从 packet/shard 出现顺序收敛为 reviewer lifecycle priority。replacement executor 在较早 packet 仍处于上游 source capture、较晚 packet 已可 ready-results intake，或 prompt repair/recovery 与 downstream ready 并存时，第一屏会优先显示最能解除 reviewer writeback closure 或必须先修复的 current action，同时保留其它 packet/shard 的 read-only provenance 与 no-spawn/no-heavy/no-authority 边界。

已实现内容：`MissionCommanderNextActionsWithReviewerDispatches` 现在在 packet representative 生成后调用 `orderReviewerDispatchMissionActions`，按 reviewer lifecycle priority 稳定排序后再合入 ordinary lane continuation；priority 为 owner adoption → prompt repair / recovery finalize → recovery preview / ambiguous disposition → ready batch intake → collection / disposed collection → staging → source capture → blocked invalid/provenance states → waiting/default。`reviewerDispatchActionPriority` 同步从粗粒度 blocked/ready/default 改为 lifecycle-aware priority；`limitReviewerDispatchIntakeHandoffs` 不再用固定初始 priority，避免 expanded priority range 让高价值旧项在 max row limit 下被错误丢弃。新增 workstream tests 锁定下游 ready intake 会排在较早 source capture 前，prompt repair 会排在 intake 前，同时保留单 packet representative 和 bounded start/reconcile priority。新增 CLI product-path E2E 构造同一 feature lane 两个 canonical reviewer packets：较早 packet 的 reviewer JSON 只写到 deterministic input path，形成 source-capture-ready；较晚 packet 经 capture → staging → collection 进入 `ready-for-reviewer-intake-preview`。测试覆盖 `status -Format json`、lane `handoff -WhatIf -Format json` 与 blocked `continue login -Apply -Format json` 都把 current action 指向较晚 ready intake packet，并断言 blocked continue zero-write、不创建 run artifacts。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不自动执行 reviewer work、source capture、staging、collection、intake、recovery、prompt repair 或 lane continue，不改变 reviewer packet/result durable schema，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；所有 mutation 仍来自既有 reviewer product-path 的显式 WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或测试临时 case artifacts。

### Batch 670：authorized adapter multi-gate queue closure

状态：已完成 authorized adapter multi-gate queue closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused 本机验证、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `fe538be` 已推送。Push run `30278768997` completed failure，Windows/Linux/macOS jobs `90019496360`/`90019496444`/`90019496490` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批把同一 lane 多个 authorized-gate adapter report handoff 的 current action 选择从“按 handoff/action 出现顺序”收敛为 adapter lifecycle priority：invalid/drift live sidecar 的 `repair-adapter-report` 优先于 record-ready action；已 acknowledgement 的 recorded adapter report 只保留 provenance，不重新进入 current/next action。这样 replacement executor 在旧 gate 已 acknowledged、新 gate drift/malformed 或另一个 report record-ready 并存时，第一屏会先修复会阻塞 evidence closure 的 invalid report，而不是复制旧 recorded/acknowledged 或较早 record command。

目标：构造同一 lane 内多 authorized gates 的真实 CLI product-path：旧 gate 已完成 hash-bound adapter report record 并由 verification acknowledgement 关闭 evidence review，新 gate 的 canonical adapter report 在 record 前 drift/malformed。`status` / `handoff` / `continue` / run status / digest / lane RESUME / checkpoint 应同时保留旧 acknowledged provenance 与新 invalid sidecar repair handoff，并把 Mission Commander current action 绑定到新 gate 的 `repair-adapter-report` / `move-evidence-refs-under-authorized-output-paths`，不能因为旧 recorded/acknowledged report 或其它 record-ready handoff 遮蔽 repair。Apply 路径仍必须 zero heavy-tool、zero authority/confirmed，repair projection 不自动修改 sidecar、不 record evidence。

已实现内容：`MissionCommanderNextActionsWithAuthorizedGateAdaptersAndAcknowledgements` 在把 adapter actions 注入 queue 前调用 `orderAuthorizedGateAdapterActions`，稳定排序 priority 为 blocked adapter action → `repair-adapter-report` → `ready-to-record-evidence` → validation-required / drafted / scaffolded → draft/scaffold apply → other → `evidence-already-recorded`；acknowledged exact recorded reports 仍在注入前跳过，因此只保留 handoff provenance。新增 workstream unit test 锁定 repair action 会优先于 record-ready，且 acknowledged recorded adapter action 不回流。扩展 no-pack nested output workspace CLI E2E：在 session-1 adapter report 完成 record + verification acknowledgement 后，session-2 case-relative report 先进入 record-ready，再 drift 为 evidenceRefs 越界的 invalid sidecar；测试覆盖 `status` JSON/text、`handoff main` WhatIf/Apply JSON/text、`continue main` WhatIf/Apply JSON/text、run `status.json`、`digest.md`、lane `RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 同时保留旧 acknowledged provenance 与新 repair handoff，并断言 current action 始终选择 session-2 repair、不暴露旧 evidence review/duplicate record current、不写 facts authority/confirmed。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 authorized-gate / execution report / acknowledgement durable schema，不自动执行 adapter/heavy tool，不自动 repair/draft/scaffold/validate/record/acknowledge，不删除或覆盖 sidecar，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；所有 mutation 仍来自既有 gate/note/continue/handoff 的显式 WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 或 read-only projection，测试写入仅限临时 case adapter sidecar 与 `.rekit` artifacts。

### Batch 669：pack-memory multi-receipt current-action closure

状态：已完成 pack-memory multi-receipt current-action closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused 本机验证、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `144ce18` 已推送。Push run `30271405181` completed failure，Linux/macOS/Windows jobs `89994581120`/`89994581241`/`89994581260` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。本批修复同一 pack 存在多个 candidate decision verification receipts 时的 Mission Commander current action 选择，避免较早的 provisioning-required receipt 遮蔽更靠后的 verification/retirement closure。完整本机 `go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-run -Format text` 已通过，返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release run ok`，聚合 `passed=7 failed=0 skipped=0`，覆盖 `release-check`、`status`、`packs`、`doctor`、`go test ./...`、`go vet ./...` 与 `git diff --check`。

目标：当 pack-memory candidate decision Apply 后产生多个 verification receipts，且 receipts 处在不同阶段（provision required / provision in-progress / verification run / retirement required / retirement in-progress）时，`release-check`、`status` 与 project handoff 的 Mission Commander action queue 必须选择最能解除 downstream closure 的 current action：进行中的 resume action 优先，其后按 verification lifecycle 的更靠后阶段优先；proof follow-up 和 inventory evidence 仍保留为 read-only provenance，不自动 merge/cleanup/provision/verify/retire/reconsume。

已实现内容：`packMemoryCandidateActiveDecisionReceipt` 从“按 receipt 顺序逐条检查所有阶段”改为“按 stage priority 扫描全部 receipts”，优先级为 retirement in-progress → provisioning in-progress → retirement required → verification run required → provisioning required。这样较早 receipt 的 fresh provisioning preview 不会挡住较晚 receipt 的 retirement preview；如果某个 receipt 已经处于 provisioning in-progress，则仍优先恢复该 expected-hash Apply，而不是切到别的 preview。新增 `TestPackMemoryCandidateActionQueuePrioritizesAdvancedReceiptAcrossReceipts` 锁定纯 action queue priority，并扩展 `TestReleaseHandoffPackMemoryCandidateDecisionVerificationReceipt` 的真实 inventory fixture，加入第二个 pending provisioning receipt，断言 release handoff 多 receipt queue 仍选择 retirement-required current action，并在 reasons 中绑定正确 receipt/provision/retirement status。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 candidate decision receipt / verification proof / retirement artifact schema，不自动 merge/cleanup/provision/verify/retire/reconsume，不删除 verification workspace 或 candidate residue，不执行 heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；所有 mutation 仍必须由既有 promote WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 命令显式触发，本批只修正 read-only Mission Commander handoff 的 current action selection。

### Batch 667：adapter report drift repair continuation closure

状态：已完成 adapter report drift repair continuation closure 的 runtime/test/doc 实现、focused adapter product-path regression、完整本机 release-run、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `31935d0` 已推送。Push run `30267170554` completed failure，Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `89980532250`/`89980532260`/`89980532290` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。Batch 653-655 已证明 authorized output workspace 可以从 execution report contract scaffold → draft → validate → hash-bound record，Batch 664 已证明 evidence acknowledgement 后 recorded adapter handoff 会变成 provenance-only；本批补齐 canonical adapter report 在 record 前 drift/malformed 或 validation fail-closed 后的 replacement executor first-screen continuation：invalid report 是 unrecorded/non-authoritative，下一步是修复 bounded sidecar、重新运行 read-only validation，成功后再使用 validation/status 返回的 exact `-ExpectedExecutionReportSha256` record。

目标：当 authorized adapter report sidecar 在 bounded output workspace 中 drift/malformed 或 validation invalid 时，Mission Commander 应在 `status`、lane/project `handoff`、`continue -WhatIf/-Apply` 和 durable handoff/continue artifacts 中给 replacement executor 一个明确的 repair/refresh path：invalid report 是 unrecorded/non-authoritative，下一步是 rerun `gate -ExecutionReportContract` / draft or scaffold apply / `-ValidateExecutionReport`，成功后再 hash-bound record；不能把 invalid report 当 recorded evidence，也不能自动修复、自动 record 或写 authority/confirmed。

### Batch 666：reviewer packet regeneration after retirement closure

状态：已完成 reviewer packet regeneration after retirement closure 的 runtime/test 实现、focused reviewer regressions、完整本机 release minimum、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `43e3fe2` 已推送。Push run `30263886763` completed failure，Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `89969904400`/`89969904464`/`89969904501` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。Batch 665 已让 invalid canonical reviewer packet 在 exact retirement 后不再阻塞 dispatch/intake，并通过 read-only retirement projection 保留 closed provenance；本批进一步证明 replacement executor 如果仍有 reviewer work，可以显式运行新的 packet generation path，并在只看 `status` / `handoff` / `continue` / durable handoff artifact 时看见“旧 retired packet 是 closed provenance，新 regenerated packet 是当前可调度 reviewer work”，而不是误把 retired provenance 当作仍需修复的 packet，或在新旧 packet 并存时丢失 current dispatch handoff。

目标：当 reviewer packet 已因 malformed bytes 被 `-RetireInvalidReviewerPacket` exact Apply 关闭后，Mission Commander 应能通过显式新 `plan-subagents` generation 生成新的 canonical reviewer packet；随后 `status`、lane/project `handoff`、`continue -WhatIf/-Apply`、run `status.json`、`digest.md`、lane `RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 应同时保持两类语义：retired old packet 只作为 `ReviewerPacketRetirementHandoffs` closed provenance 保留，regenerated new packet 进入 `ReviewerDispatchIntakeHandoffs` / action queue / current reviewer dispatch runbook，replacement executor 可继续从新 packet dispatch reviewer 或等待 reviewer result，而不是继续尝试 retire/repair old packet。

已实现内容：扩展 reviewer packet retirement CLI product-path E2E：在 invalid packet retirement Apply 和 closed continue artifacts 写入后，显式用新的 `.rekit/reviews/regenerated-after-retirement` output dir 重新运行 `plan-subagents` 生成 fresh canonical packet。测试断言 regenerated packet path / packetId 与 retired packet 不同；`status -Format json/text` 同时保留旧 `ReviewerPacketRetirementHandoffs` closed provenance，并让新 packet 的 `ReviewerDispatchIntakeHandoffs` 成为 reviewer-only queue 与 case Mission Commander current action，state=`waiting-for-reviewer-result`，不再暴露旧 packet 的 `-RetireInvalidReviewerPacket` preview。lane `handoff -WhatIf/-Apply` JSON 与 `.rekit/handovers/feature-review-latest.md` 也同时保留旧 retirement provenance 与新 dispatch handoff。`continue review -WhatIf/-Apply` 在新 reviewer work open 时保持 blocked/zero-write，但 JSON 与 text 都显示旧 retirement provenance 和新 dispatch next action；本批最小修复 `writeContinueText` 的 reviewer-dispatch blocked 分支，使其像 JSON/durable projections 一样打印 `ReviewerPacketRetirementHandoffs`，避免 replacement executor 在 blocked continue text 中丢失旧 retired packet closure。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 regenerate reviewer packet，不修改或删除 retired packet、`packet.retirement.json` 或 `packet.integrity.json`，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不自动 collect/intake reviewer result，不写 verification/decision/authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；regeneration 必须是显式 `plan-subagents` generation，retired packet 仍只能作为 read-only closed provenance。

状态：已完成 reviewer invalid packet retirement durable continuation closure 的 runtime/test 实现、focused reviewer retirement regressions、完整本机 release minimum、implementation push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `5f09aa9` 已推送。Push run `30262520469` completed failure，Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `89965506516`/`89965506535`/`89965506569` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍是既有 runner/billing blocker；未发现新的远程 release signal，不声明远程绿色。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 retire/regenerate reviewer packet，不删除或修复 invalid packet bytes，不修改 packet.integrity，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不自动 reviewer intake/continue，不写 verification/decision/authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；retirement 仍只能由显式 WhatIf → expected packet/integrity hash Apply 写入 exact receipt；新增 retirement projection 只读地表达 closed provenance，不迁移 retirement receipt schema，也不把 retired packet 塞回 dispatch/intake handoff。

### Batch 664：authorized adapter evidence acknowledgement closure

状态：已完成 authorized adapter evidence acknowledgement closure 的 runtime/test 实现、本机 release-run、implementation push 与远程 release-gate inspection。adapter execution evidence acknowledgement 关闭 `executionEvidenceReview` queue 后，authorized gate adapter handoff 现在保留 recorded adapter report provenance，同时显式标记 acknowledged/closed，并清除可复制的 review/record current action，避免 replacement executor 把已关闭 evidence review 或 duplicate record snapshot 误当作下一步。

目标：当 bounded adapter execution report 已经通过 `gate -Apply -ExecutionReportPath ... -ExpectedExecutionReportSha256 ...` 记录 observation evidence，随后 verification acknowledgement note 关闭该 evidence review 后，`status`、lane/project `handoff`、`continue -WhatIf/-Apply`、run `status.json`、`digest.md`、lane `RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 应保持同一语义：`executionEvidenceReview` 为 0，Mission Commander current action 不来自 `executionEvidenceReview` 或 `adapterReportLiveSnapshot.recordedEvidence`，authorized adapter handoff 仍可审计 report path/hash/adapter/output refs，但标记为 acknowledged/closed 且不打印可复制的 record/review action。

已实现内容：`AuthorizedGateAdapterHandoffsWithAcknowledgements` 以 `ExecutionEvidenceReviewAcknowledgedIDs` 为输入生成 acknowledgement-aware adapter handoff，原 `AuthorizedGateAdapterHandoffs` 保留为兼容 wrapper；overview、status、handoff、continue、start、reconcile 与 lane resume/checkpoint call path 已切到 acknowledgement-aware projection。acknowledged recorded handoff 新增 `acknowledged=true` / `acknowledgementState=execution-evidence-review-acknowledged`，保留 report path/hash、adapter/live validation provenance 与 scaffold/validate contract provenance，但清空 `reportSummary.currentAction`、`nextActionCount`、`reviewRequiredActionCount`、`actionQueueSummary`、live `recordCommand` / `caseRelativeRecordCommand`，并把 record/caseRecord 文本与 Markdown 改为 `closed: execution evidence review acknowledged; no record action remains`。acknowledged projection 同时过滤 recorded summary 中已关闭的 `review outputRefs/evidenceRefs` / `ready-for-evidence-review` boundary/runbook，只保留 provenance-only/no-review/no-record/no-replay closure boundary。CLI no-pack adapter product-path E2E 的 acknowledgement 段现在断言 `status` JSON/text、`handoff` JSON/text、`continue` WhatIf/Apply JSON/text、run `status.json`、`digest.md`、lane `RESUME.md` 与 checkpoint 都保留 acknowledged adapter provenance，且不暴露已关闭 review 或 record current action。

状态：已完成 pack-memory downstream proof follow-up closure 的 runtime/test 实现、本机 release-run、implementation push 与远程 release-gate inspection；candidate decision Apply 后如果 verification/provisioning current action 与 cleanup/reconsume proof 缺失同时存在，`status` / `release-check` 现在会保留 verification current action，同时把缺失的 cleanup/reconsume proof draft 暴露为可复制的 Mission Commander follow-up action，避免 replacement executor 在 verification 队列和 proof summary 之间手工拼接 downstream closure。

目标：Batch 659 已把 pack-memory proof/verification/provision/retirement 阶段提升为 first-screen current action，但 accepted/rejected candidate decision Apply 后会同时出现 verification current 与 cleanup proof missing；旧行为只能从 `proofSummary.nextMissingProof` 推断 cleanup proof draft，当前 action 被 verification receipt 占用。Batch 661 补齐 downstream UX：verification/provision/retirement 仍保持 current，不被 proof draft 抢占；cleanup/reconsume proof 以 stage-specific `pack-memory-*-proof-required` follow-up action 留在 queue，status runbook 明确这是 proof follow-up 而非 current action，并在 proof Apply 后自动移除。

已实现内容：`packMemoryCandidateNextActions` 拆分 primary current action 与 proof follow-up action；当 `ProofSummary.NextMissingProof.DraftCommand` 存在但不同于 current command 时，新增 `packMemoryCandidateProofFollowUpAction`，按 `decision-proof-required` / `cleanup-proof-required` / `reconsume-proof-required` 生成 `pack-memory-decision-proof-required`、`pack-memory-cleanup-proof-required` 或 `pack-memory-reconsume-proof-required` actionId，source 标记为 `.followUp.proof`，并保留 proof stage/type/path/candidate/packTarget、read-only handoff、no-auto-mutation 与 hash-gated Apply boundary。`status` first-screen pack-memory runbook 现在区分 proof draft 是 current 还是 follow-up，避免在 verification current 存在时误写“from current pack-memory action”。CLI product-path E2E 在 `promote` candidate decision Apply 后、cleanup proof Apply 前断言 `status` 与 `release-check` current action 仍指向 `verificationProvisionCommand`，同时 queue 含 `pack-memory-cleanup-proof-required` follow-up；随后执行 cleanup proof WhatIf/Apply，并断言 proof follow-up 消失、queue 回到单一 verification current。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 merge/cleanup/provision/verify/retire/reconsume，不执行 heavy tool，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；新增 action queue 仍是 read-only Mission Commander handoff，所有 mutation 仍必须由既有 `promote -DraftReviewProof -WhatIf` 与 `-ExpectedProofSha256 -Apply`、candidate verification provisioning/verification/retirement 的显式 WhatIf → expected-hash Apply 触发。

### Batch 658：reviewer malformed input semantic fail-closed closure

状态：已完成 reviewer malformed input semantic fail-closed closure 的 shared reviewer-result contract validator、workstream status semantic input classification、CLI product-path 测试实现、focused malformed input recovery regression、相邻 reviewer/status regression、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `77dd4e8` 已推送。Push run `30240985134` completed failure，Linux/macOS/Windows jobs `89897949024`/`89897949045`/`89897949128` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：Batch 657 已证明 empty/invalid file 会让 `status` / Mission Commander queue 进入 `reviewer-result-input-invalid`，并能修正同一路径后通过 source capture WhatIf → `-ExpectedReviewerResultInputSha256` hash-bound Apply 发布 packet-derived source，再写 durable source-ready handoff；但 non-empty malformed reviewer JSON 目前只通过 non-empty regular-file 分类，容易被 `status` 误判为 `ready-for-reviewer-result-source-capture-preview`，直到 source capture preview 才失败。replacement executor 需要在第一屏 `status` 阶段就看到 malformed reviewer input blocker，避免把坏 JSON 当成可 capture 的 reviewer result 继续推进。

### Batch 656：reviewer prompt artifact repair durable continuation closure

状态：已完成 reviewer prompt artifact repair durable continuation closure 的 CLI product-path 测试实现、focused reviewer repair regression、相邻 reviewer/status regression、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `b43292b` 已推送。Push run `30238493212` completed failure，Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `89890764024`/`89890764052`/`89890764083` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：执行 Batch 655 后用户新增约束，停止 adapter report lifecycle 连续微批，转向 reviewer orchestration 的真实接手断点。`plan-subagents` 已生成 reviewer packet、dispatch prompt artifact、source capture/staging/collection/intake handoff；但当 canonical reviewer dispatch prompt artifact 缺失或在会话/文件操作中丢失时，replacement executor 需要能从 packet/status 发现 repair blocker，运行 `-RepairReviewerPromptArtifact -WhatIf` 复原 packet-derived prompt bytes，再用 hash-bound Apply 恢复 prompt artifact，随后让 `status` / Mission Commander queue 回到 read-only reviewer dispatch，并通过 `handoff` durable lane artifact 写入保留恢复后的 reviewer dispatch/intake handoff，避免 reviewer session 因 prompt artifact drift/missing 卡死。

已实现内容：扩展 `TestRunPlanSubagentsReviewerOrchestrationE2E` 的同一临时 case product path。测试在 `plan-subagents` 生成 reviewer packet、dispatch prompt artifact 与 reviewer dispatch/intake handoff 后，删除 `shard-01` canonical dispatch prompt artifact，确认 `status -Format json/text` fail-closed 到 `reviewer-dispatch-prompt-artifact-invalid`、`promptArtifactBlocked=1` 与 `-RepairReviewerPromptArtifact` current action；随后运行 repair WhatIf，断言 read-only/no-write preview 返回 packet-derived prompt SHA-256 与 hash-bound Apply command；再用 `-ExpectedPromptSha256` 执行 Apply，确认只恢复 exact prompt bytes。新增断言证明 repair 后 `status -Format json` 重新把 shard 恢复为 `waiting-for-reviewer-result`、`dispatchPromptCurrent=true`、`promptArtifactBlocked=0`，current action 不再是 repair blocker；随后运行 `handoff login -Apply -Format json`，验证 durable lane handoff 写入 `.rekit/handovers/feature-login-latest.md`，其 Markdown 保留 `Reviewer dispatch intake handoff`、ready prompt hash、read-only reviewer dispatch command 与 no-spawn/no-monitor boundary，且不再包含 prompt artifact missing/repair blocker。后续测试继续覆盖 `continue -WhatIf`、blocked `continue -Apply` zero-write、source capture/staging/collection/intake 与最终 reviewer provenance/no authority/confirmed 断言。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不执行 reviewer work、不执行 heavy tool、不写 verification/decision/authority/confirmed，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；repair Apply 只允许在测试临时 case 的 canonical reviewer prompt artifact path 恢复 packet-derived exact bytes，并必须由 WhatIf 返回的 prompt SHA-256 绑定；durable handoff 只写测试临时 case 的 lane handover Markdown，不替代 reviewer dispatch、collection 或 intake。

### Batch 651：reviewer deterministic input drop-path handoff closure

状态：已完成 reviewer input drop path runtime / status-handoff projection、CLI/workstream 回归、用户可见 docs/skill 更新、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `5378e33` 已推送。Push run `30231525827` completed failure，Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `89871203547`/`89871203622`/`89871203630` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：继续执行端到端能力闭环约束，转向 reviewer dispatch 中“等待 reviewer JSON”阶段的 replacement executor 接手断点。Batch 641/643 已证明主 Agent 应先保存 reviewer JSON 到 case-local input，再 source capture→stage→collect→intake；但 fresh packet/status/handoff 仍会暴露 `<case-local-reviewer-json-input>` 或“symlink-free case-local input”式占位，让主 Agent / replacement executor 必须手工决定保存路径。新会话在 reviewer 返回 JSON 后应能从 packet/status/handoff/current action 直接复制 deterministic input path 与 source capture command。

已实现内容：`plan-subagents` 现在为 canonical collection-capable shard 派生 `reviewerStagingCommands.sourceCaptureInput = .rekit/reviews/<review-root>/results/inputs/<shard>.reviewer-input.json`，创建 `results/inputs/` artifact namespace，并把 source capture preview/apply command 中的 `-ReviewerResultInputPath` 绑定到该 concrete path；dispatch prompt、reviewer orchestration scope/lifecycle、shard next action、summary text 和 CLI text 同步提示保存到 `reviewerStagingCommands.sourceCaptureInput`。`ReviewerDispatchIntakeHandoff` / summary 新增 `reviewerResultInputPath` / `reviewerResultInputState`、`latest/nextActionReviewerResultInput*`，status/handoff/continue evidence、boundary、runbook 和 Markdown/text 输出显示 input path/state；状态机新增 `ready-for-reviewer-result-source-capture-preview` 与 `reviewer-result-input-invalid`，Mission Commander action queue 将 source-capture preview 视为可执行 review action，同时 source-ready staging 仍优先于 input-ready，避免 capture 完成后被仍存在的 input 文件挡住 staging。CLI/workstream tests 改为消费 packet-derived input path，覆盖 missing input → ready source capture preview → ready staging preview → collection/batch intake、forged source/input binding fail-closed，以及 installed entrypoint first-screen runbook 文案更新；`docs/agent-team-usage.md` 与 `/rekit` skill guidance 不再把 canonical case-local packet 的 input path交给执行者手工选择。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 reviewer result durable schema，不自动 spawn/monitor reviewer，不自动执行 source capture/staging/collection/intake，不写 verification/decision/authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；旧 packet 的 `<case-local-reviewer-json-input>` 仅作为兼容 fallback sentinel 被识别，不出现在新 canonical packet/status/handoff 的 runnable command 中。

### Batch 650：pack-memory candidate verification proof replay handoff closure

状态：已完成 candidate verification proof Apply replay runtime 修正、case-local pack-memory product-path 回归扩展、focused CLI/promote regressions、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `7402946` 已推送。Push run `30228542128` completed failure，Windows/macOS/Linux jobs `89862991416`/`89862991443`/`89862991467` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：继续执行端到端能力闭环约束，转向 pack-memory verification proof 已写入后的 replacement executor 接手断点。现有 candidate verification Apply 已用 idempotent durable write 防止重写 proof，但返回 envelope 与 text 无法区分首次 proof 写入和 exact proof 已存在的 zero-write replay；会话中断后 executor 重跑同一 `-VerifyCandidateDecision -Apply` 时，容易把已完成 verification 误读成新 proof 写入，或不确定下一步是否应进入 retirement preview。durable verification proof 仍必须保持 authority/evidence 语义，不持久化运行时 handoff 字段。

已实现内容：`CandidateDecisionVerificationResult` 新增返回级 `mode` / `replay`；`VerifyCandidateDecision` 的 WhatIf 返回 `mode=previewed`，首次 Apply 返回 `mode=verified`，exact proof bytes 已存在时返回 `mode=already-verified` / `replay=true`，保留 `retirementPreviewCommand` 后续 handoff，并明确 next step 为 exact candidate verification proof already exists。`writeDurableFileIdempotent` 改为返回是否命中 already-existing exact bytes；proof 文件写入仍使用不含 `mode` / `replay` / `verificationRunbookSteps` 的 `proofResult`，避免把 transient operational handoff 写入 durable proof authority。CLI text 同步打印 `mode` 与 `replay`。扩展 `TestRunPromoteCandidateDecisionCaseLocalPreviewAndApply`：verification preview → JSON Apply → JSON replay → text replay，断言首次 Apply 为 `verified`、replay 为 `already-verified`、candidate/workspace 快照不变、durable proof 不持久化 transient fields，并继续进入既有 retirement required / retirement apply / status / release-check closure。

边界：本批不新增 public command，不改变 verification proof durable authority schema，不自动 run provisioning/verification/retirement/reconsume，不写 authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；唯一 runtime 变更是把现有 idempotent proof write 的 replay 状态显式投影到返回 envelope/text，保持 replay zero-write 和后续 retirement handoff 清晰。

边界：本批不改变 repair/runtime 语义，不新增 public command，不让 repair 写 board/facts/lanes，不运行 managed docs sync Apply，不写 authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic；唯一新增能力是把现有 moved-case repair 恢复路径锁定成 CLI 端到端回归，证明 repair 是 attach/init/sync/doctor 产品路径的 review-first 恢复门。

### Batch 626：release-run transient retry downstream truthfulness closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 工作树实现、focused release handoff/CLI 回归、releasecheck/CLI package validation、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `1a1f068` 已推送。Push run `30209344354` completed failure，macOS/Linux/Windows jobs `89812835298`/`89812835308`/`89812835325` 均 `steps=[]` / `runner_id=0` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

- `ReleaseHandoffLatestBatchHandoff` 新增只读 `validationWarnings[]`；`statusProjectHandoff` 同步投影为 `latestValidationWarnings[]`。
- latest-batch parser 只在文本明确包含 `transientRetryReason`、`release-run step retry` 或 `attempts=2` 时识别 retry；普通 cleanup-lock 叙述、Batch 625 的 `attempts=1` 与“未触发 retry”不会误报。
- 已记录 retry 的 completed release-run 会保留 `localValidationReady=true` / `releaseCheckReady=true`，但 evidence 新增 `release-run transient retry recorded`，validation warning 提醒 review retry reason 与 first-attempt output；retry 后仍 `ready=false` / `failed=1` 的 release-run 保持 local validation not-ready，并继续指向重跑 full local release minimum。
- `release-check -Format text` 与 `status -Format text` 新增 latest batch validation warning 行；JSON 可供 automation 消费同一 warning。

边界：本批只增强 release readiness/status 的只读 truthfulness projection；不改变 `release-run` 执行/retry 语义，不改变 gateProfile/recommendedMinimum，不把 transient retry 当 remote green，不写 repo/case state，不联网读取 GitHub Actions，不写 authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。

### Batch 625：release-run Windows Go test cleanup retry closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 工作树实现、focused release-run CLI 回归、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `db580f9` 已推送。Push run `30208604009` completed failure，Linux/macOS/Windows jobs `89810907570`/`89810907614`/`89810907617` 均 `steps=[]` / `runner_id=0` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：补齐 Windows 本机 release verification 的真实稳定性断点：Batch 624 的首次完整 `release-run` 已看到 `go test ./...` 所有 package 输出 `ok`，但 Go 在清理临时 `*.test.exe` 时被 Windows 文件占用锁阻塞，导致整次本机 release minimum 误报 failed。维护者必须人工判断并重跑，容易把 transient cleanup lock 和真实测试失败混在一起。本批让 `release-run` 对该特定 cleanup-lock 形态做一次可审计重试，同时保持真实测试失败 fail-closed。

已实现内容：

- `releaseRunStepResult` 新增只读 retry metadata：`attempts`、`transientRetryReason`、`firstAttemptExitCode`、`firstAttemptError` 与 `firstAttemptOutputTail`，text 输出同步打印 `release-run step retry` 和 first-attempt tail 行。
- `go test ./...` step 首次失败时，只有输出/错误同时匹配 Windows `go: unlinkat ... .test.exe ... used by another process` cleanup-lock 形态，且没有 `FAIL` / setup failed / build failed / panic 等真实测试失败信号，才会重试一次；其它命令、普通 go test failure、build/setup failure 和 retry 后失败仍按原 release-run failure 语义记录。
- Tests 覆盖 cleanup lock first attempt → retry success、真实 go test failure + cleanup lock 不重试、普通 go test failure 不重试，以及既有 release-run step order / failure aggregation / release inspection handoff。

边界：本批只增强 Go-native `release-run` 的 Windows 本机验证稳定性与审计输出；不新增 public command，不改变 gateProfile/recommendedMinimum，不跳过任何 release step，不吞掉真实测试失败，不写 repo/case state，不联网读取 GitHub Actions，不写 authority/confirmed，不执行 heavy tool，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。

验证结果：focused `go test ./internal/rekit/cli -run "TestRunReleaseRun" -count=1` 已通过。完整本机 `go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-run -Format text` 已通过，返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release run ok`，聚合执行 `release-check`、`status`、`packs`、`doctor`、`go test ./...`、`go vet ./...`、`git diff --check` 7 步，`passed=7 failed=0 skipped=0`；本次 `go test ./...` step 为 `attempts=1`，未触发 cleanup-lock retry；`git diff --check` 仅保留 Windows 工作树 LF→CRLF 提示。implementation commit `db580f9` 已推送。Push-triggered release-gate run `30208604009` completed failure，Linux/macOS/Windows jobs `89810907570`/`89810907614`/`89810907617` 均为既有 `steps=[]` / `runner_id=0` blocker，不能声明 remote CI green。

### Batch 624：adapter execution report lifecycle runbook handoff closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 工作树实现、focused adapter gate/CLI 回归、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `c631cdc` 已推送。Push run `30207891944` completed failure，Linux/Windows/macOS jobs `89809035554`/`89809035577`/`89809035578` 均 `steps=[]` / `runner_id=0` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：补齐 adapter execution report lifecycle 的真实接手断点：Batch 568/569/580/594/602/618 已让 sidecar scaffold/draft、read-only validation、hash-bound record currentness 与 action identity 形成闭环，但 replacement executor 执行 contract、scaffold、draft、validation、status handoff 或 record 后，仍需要从 `nextSteps[]`、Mission Commander command、path/hash 和 boundary 中手工拼接下一条 bounded operation。本批让每个 adapter report lifecycle envelope 自身携带可复制 runbook，直接说明确认当前 state/path/hash、先 validation、只使用 validation/status 返回的 expected hash record Apply、record 后转 evidence review 的顺序。

已实现内容：

- `AdapterExecutionReportContract`、`AdapterExecutionReportScaffold`、`AdapterExecutionReportDraft`、`AdapterReportLiveValidation`、`AdapterExecutionReportValidation` 与 execution evidence `ApplyResult` 新增只读 `runbookSteps[]`，由当前 lifecycle stage/state、report path、report SHA-256、Mission Commander command、next steps 与 boundary guard 派生并去重。
- Contract/live validation/scaffold/draft/validation/recorded-evidence snapshot/record result 均会输出 runbook；valid record-ready 分支明确要求替换 `<executor-id>` 并使用 validation/status envelope 中的 `-ExpectedExecutionReportSha256`，invalid/missing/malformed 分支保留 repair handoff 与 do-not-record boundary，record/duplicate 分支提示先 review outputRefs/evidenceRefs 再判断 authority/confirmed。
- CLI text 现在打印 `gate adapter report ... runbook` 与 `gate execution evidence runbook` 行；`status` 与 workstream authorized-gate live validation handoff JSON/text 复制同一 `runbookSteps[]`，让 replacement executor 从 default/status/overview/handoff/start/reconcile/continue 接手时不必回查完整 contract 或 sidecar。
- Focused tests 覆盖 contract、scaffold、draft、validation valid/invalid/missing、recorded evidence snapshot、bounded evidence record result、CLI text 输出与 project/lane handoff JSON runbook 投影。

边界：本批只增强 adapter execution report lifecycle 的只读 operational handoff 与测试；不新增 public command，不执行 adapter/heavy tool，不自动 validate/record，不放宽 expected-hash Apply currentness，不写 authority/confirmed，不新增 durable schema，不新增 PowerShell runtime logic。

### Batch 623：reviewer result collection recovery handoff closure

状态：已完成 runtime/test/doc 工作树实现、focused reviewer collection/recovery 回归、recovery disposition fail-closed 回归修正、完整本机 `release-run` release minimum、implementation commit/push 与 push-triggered remote release-gate inspection；implementation commit `84fe8af` 已推送。Push run `30205009789` completed failure，macOS/Windows/Linux jobs `89801445817`/`89801445818`/`89801445833` 均 `steps=[]` 且无 logs，仍属既有 runner/billing blocker；不为 release inspection record 自身追加第三个 inspection。

目标：补齐 reviewer result collection 冲突恢复的真实产品断点：Batch 549/558/600/622 已让 reviewer source capture、staging、collection 和 runbook 都可显式接手，但当 packet-derived candidate 已准备好、canonical reviewer result path 被不同 bytes 或可恢复 obstruction 占据时，`-CollectReviewerResult -WhatIf` 仍只返回普通 error（例如 refusing overwrite / non-empty regular file），replacement executor 需要人工知道要切到 `-RecoverReviewerResult -WhatIf`、补 reason、复核 hashes、Apply 后再重跑 collection。本批让 collection preview 自身在可恢复冲突时返回结构化 recovery handoff，同时保持 Apply fail-closed。

已实现内容：

- `ReviewerResultCollectionResult` 新增只读 conflict snapshot 字段：`recoveryRequired`、`reviewerResultKind`、`reviewerResultBytes`、`reviewerResultMode` 与 `reviewerResultLinkTarget`，用于描述阻塞 canonical result 的 exact kind/hash/size/mode，而不读取或覆盖 candidate 之外的内容。
- `CollectReviewerResult -WhatIf` 在正常 collection preflight 遇到不同 canonical bytes、empty-file 或 symlink obstruction 时，改为返回 `status=recovery-required` / `recoveryRequired=true`，并把 current Mission Commander action 指向 bounded `/rekit plan-subagents -RecoverReviewerResult ... -WhatIf -Format json`；runbook 继续要求先跑 recovery WhatIf、复核 exact hashes、只用返回的 hash-bound Apply，再重新跑 collection WhatIf。
- `-CollectReviewerResult -Apply` 保持原有 no-overwrite fail-closed；collection preview 不 quarantine、不删除、不写 receipt、不执行 recovery、不写 facts/authority/confirmed。
- CLI text 的 collection artifact 行新增 canonical kind/bytes，case-local obstruction recovery E2E 先从 collection WhatIf 看到 `recovery-required` handoff，再执行既有 Recover WhatIf→hash-bound Apply→collection→ready intake 链路。

边界：本批只增强 reviewer result collection 的可恢复冲突 WhatIf handoff 与测试；不自动执行 recovery，不改变 `-RecoverReviewerResult` 的 expected-hash Apply、quarantine/receipt 语义，不放宽 canonical directory / unsupported obstruction fail-closed，不改变 reviewer intake、facts/ledger、authority/confirmed、heavy-tool 或 PowerShell runtime logic。

验证结果：focused `go test ./internal/rekit/subagents ./internal/rekit/cli -run "TestCollectReviewerResultRejectsBindingsCollisionAndSymlink|TestRunPlanSubagentsReviewerResultObstructionRecoveryCaseLocalE2E" -count=1` 已通过；disposition regression focused `go test ./internal/rekit/subagents ./internal/rekit/cli -run "TestCollectReviewerResultRejectsBindingsCollisionAndSymlink|TestRetireAmbiguousReviewerResultRecoveryRetainsCanonical|TestRunPlanSubagentsReviewerResultObstructionRecoveryCaseLocalE2E" -count=1` 已通过。Package validation `go test ./internal/rekit/subagents ./internal/rekit/cli -count=1` 已通过。完整本机 `go run ./cmd/rekit -- -Command release-run -Format text` 已通过，返回 `ready=true` / `summary=release run ok`，聚合执行 `release-check`、`status`、`packs`、`doctor`、`go test ./...`、`go vet ./...`、`git diff --check` 7 步，`passed=7 failed=0 skipped=0`；`git diff --check` 仅保留 Windows 工作树 LF→CRLF 提示。implementation commit `84fe8af` 已推送。Push-triggered release-gate run `30205009789` completed failure，macOS/Windows/Linux jobs `89801445817`/`89801445818`/`89801445833` 均为既有 `steps=[]` / `runner_id=0` blocker，不能声明 remote CI green。
